================================================================
FILE: Repos/MP-SPDZ/Programs/Source/auction_n.mpc
================================================================
# n-Party Sealed-Bid Auction (First-Price) with MASCOT Protocol
# Party 0 is the seller (reserve price). Parties 1..N are bidders.
# Only party 0 learns the winner ID and winning bid.
# Each other party learns only whether they won (1) or lost (0).
# Usage: compile.py auction_n -- <n>
#        ./mascot-party.x -p <id> -N <n> auction_n-<n>

from Compiler.types import sint, Array
from Compiler.library import print_ln, print_ln_to
import sys

# Get number of parties from command line
if len(program.args) > 1:
    n_parties = int(program.args[1])
else:
    n_parties = 3
    print_ln('No party count specified, using default: 3')

if n_parties < 2:
    print_ln('Error: Need at least 2 parties for MASCOT MPC')
    raise Exception('MASCOT requires at least 2 parties')

print_ln('Starting %s-party sealed-bid auction (MASCOT)', n_parties)

# Each party inputs their bid (party 0 = seller's reserve price)
bids = Array(n_parties, sint)
for i in range(n_parties):
    bids[i] = sint.get_input_from(i)
    print_ln('Party %s: bid received', i)

# Find maximum bid and winner
max_bid = bids[0]
winner = sint(0)

for i in range(1, n_parties):
    is_higher = bids[i] > max_bid
    max_bid = is_higher.if_else(bids[i], max_bid)
    winner = is_higher.if_else(sint(i), winner)

# Reveal winner and winning bid ONLY to party 0 (seller)
winner_id = winner.reveal_to(0)
winning_bid = max_bid.reveal_to(0)

print_ln_to(0, 'Auction complete!')
print_ln_to(0, 'Winner: Party %s', winner_id)
print_ln_to(0, 'Winning bid: %s', winning_bid)

# Each party privately learns only whether they won
for party in range(n_parties):
    am_i_winner = (winner == sint(party)).reveal_to(party)
    print_ln_to(party, 'You won: %s', am_i_winner)

================================================================
FILE: Repos/veilid/playground/src/main.rs
================================================================
use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};
use nix::sys::signal::{self, Signal};
use nix::unistd::Pid;
use std::io::Write;
use std::net::TcpStream;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::{Child, Command};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[derive(Parser)]
#[command(name = "veilid-playground")]
#[command(about = "Launch a local Veilid devnet without Docker")]
struct Cli {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    command: SubCommand,
}

#[derive(Subcommand)]
enum SubCommand {
    /// Start a local devnet
    Start(StartArgs),
    /// Run a command attached to the running devnet
    Exec(ExecArgs),
    /// Remove playground data directory
    Clean(CleanArgs),
}

#[derive(clap::Args)]
struct CleanArgs {
    /// Data directory to remove
    #[arg(long, default_value = "/tmp/veilid-playground")]
    data_dir: PathBuf,
}

#[derive(clap::Args)]
struct ExecArgs {
    /// Data directory (must match running playground)
    #[arg(long, default_value = "/tmp/veilid-playground")]
    data_dir: PathBuf,

    /// Command and arguments
    #[arg(trailing_var_arg = true, allow_hyphen_values = true, required = true)]
    cmd: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(clap::Args)]
struct StartArgs {
    /// Number of nodes (1 bootstrap + N-1 regular)
    #[arg(default_value = "5")]
    nodes: u16,

    /// Base port for the devnet
    #[arg(long, default_value = "5150")]
    base_port: u16,

    /// Network key for isolation
    #[arg(long, default_value = "development-network-2025")]
    network_key: String,

    /// Path to veilid-server binary (auto-detected from PATH if not set)
    #[arg(long)]
    veilid_server: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// Base data directory for node storage
    #[arg(long, default_value = "/tmp/veilid-playground")]
    data_dir: PathBuf,

    /// Path to `libveilid_ipspoof.so` (auto-detected from workspace target/ if not set)
    #[arg(long)]
    ipspoof: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// Enable debug logging on veilid-server nodes
    #[arg(long)]
    debug: bool,
}

struct NodeInfo {
    index: u16,
    port: u16,
    fake_ip: String,
    child: Child,
}

fn main() {
    let cli = Cli::parse();

    match cli.command {
        SubCommand::Start(args) => run_start(args),
        SubCommand::Exec(ref exec_args) => run_exec(exec_args),
        SubCommand::Clean(args) => run_clean(&args),
    }
}

fn run_clean(args: &CleanArgs) {
    if args.data_dir.exists() {
        eprintln!(
            "[playground] cleaning data dir: {}",
            args.data_dir.display()
        );
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&args.data_dir);
        eprintln!("[playground] done");
    } else {
        eprintln!(
            "[playground] nothing to clean: {} does not exist",
            args.data_dir.display()
        );
    }
}

fn run_start(mut args: StartArgs) {
    let veilid_server = args
        .veilid_server
        .take()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| find_in_path("veilid-server"));
    if !veilid_server.exists() {
        eprintln!(
            "error: veilid-server not found at {}. Install it or use --veilid-server.",
            veilid_server.display()
        );
        std::process::exit(1);
    }

    let ipspoof = args.ipspoof.take().unwrap_or_else(auto_detect_ipspoof);
    if !ipspoof.exists() {
        eprintln!(
            "error: libveilid_ipspoof.so not found at {}. Build ipspoof crate or use --ipspoof.",
            ipspoof.display()
        );
        std::process::exit(1);
    }

    if args.nodes < 1 {
        eprintln!("error: need at least 1 node");
        std::process::exit(1);
    }

    std::fs::create_dir_all(&args.data_dir).expect("failed to create data directory");

    let shutdown = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let shutdown_flag = shutdown.clone();
    ctrlc_handler(shutdown_flag);

    let mut nodes = launch_nodes(&args, &veilid_server, &ipspoof, &shutdown);

    write_state_file(&args, &ipspoof);

    print_status(&args, &nodes);
    run_loop(&shutdown, &mut nodes);

    eprintln!("\n[playground] shutting down...");
    cleanup(&mut nodes);
}

fn launch_nodes(
    args: &StartArgs,
    veilid_server: &Path,
    ipspoof: &Path,
    shutdown: &Arc<AtomicBool>,
) -> Vec<NodeInfo> {
    let mut nodes: Vec<NodeInfo> = Vec::new();

    eprintln!(
        "[playground] starting bootstrap node (port {})",
        args.base_port
    );
    let bootstrap = start_node(0, args, veilid_server, ipspoof, true);
    nodes.push(bootstrap);

    if !wait_for_port(args.base_port, Duration::from_secs(30), shutdown) {
        eprintln!("error: bootstrap node failed to start within 30s");
        cleanup(&mut nodes);
        std::process::exit(1);
    }
    eprintln!("[playground] bootstrap node healthy");

    for i in 1..args.nodes {
        if shutdown.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            break;
        }
        let node = start_node(i, args, veilid_server, ipspoof, false);
        eprintln!(
            "[playground] started node {i} (port {})",
            args.base_port + i
        );
        nodes.push(node);
    }

    // Wait for all regular nodes to become healthy
    for i in 1..args.nodes {
        if shutdown.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            break;
        }
        let port = args.base_port + i;
        if !wait_for_port(port, Duration::from_secs(30), shutdown) {
            eprintln!("error: node {i} (port {port}) failed to start within 30s");
            cleanup(&mut nodes);
            std::process::exit(1);
        }
    }

    nodes
}

fn print_status(args: &StartArgs, nodes: &[NodeInfo]) {
    println!();
    println!("=== Veilid Playground ===");
    println!("Nodes: {}", args.nodes);
    println!("Network key: {}", args.network_key);
    println!("Base port: {}", args.base_port);
    println!("Data dir: {}", args.data_dir.display());
    println!();
    println!("{:<6} {:<16} {:<8} {:<8}", "Node", "Fake IP", "Port", "PID");
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(42));
    for node in nodes {
        let role = if node.index == 0 { " (bootstrap)" } else { "" };
        println!(
            "{:<6} {:<16} {:<8} {:<8}{role}",
            node.index,
            node.fake_ip,
            node.port,
            node.child.id(),
        );
    }
    println!();
    println!("Press Ctrl+C to stop.");
    let _ = std::io::stdout().flush();
}

fn run_loop(shutdown: &AtomicBool, nodes: &mut [NodeInfo]) {
    while !shutdown.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
        for node in nodes.iter_mut() {
            match node.child.try_wait() {
                Ok(Some(status)) => {
                    eprintln!(
                        "[playground] node {} (pid {}) exited unexpectedly: {status}",
                        node.index,
                        node.child.id(),
                    );
                    shutdown.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
                    break;
                }
                Ok(None) => {} // still running
                Err(e) => {
                    eprintln!("[playground] error checking node {}: {e}", node.index);
                }
            }
        }
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(500));
    }
}

fn start_node(
    index: u16,
    args: &StartArgs,
    veilid_server: &Path,
    ipspoof: &Path,
    is_bootstrap: bool,
) -> NodeInfo {
    let port = args.base_port + index;
    let fake_ip = format!("1.2.3.{}", index + 1);
    let public_addr = format!("{fake_ip}:{port}");

    let mut cmd = Command::new(veilid_server);

    cmd.arg("--subnode-index").arg(index.to_string());
    cmd.arg("--network-key").arg(&args.network_key);

    if is_bootstrap {
        set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.routing_table.bootstrap", "[]");
    } else {
        set_config(
            &mut cmd,
            "core.network.routing_table.bootstrap",
            &format!("[\"udp://1.2.3.1:{}\"]", args.base_port),
        );
    }
    set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.routing_table.bootstrap_keys", "[]");

    set_config(
        &mut cmd,
        "core.network.protocol.udp.public_address",
        &format!("\"{public_addr}\""),
    );
    set_config(
        &mut cmd,
        "core.network.protocol.tcp.public_address",
        &format!("\"{public_addr}\""),
    );

    set_config(
        &mut cmd,
        "core.network.protocol.udp.listen_address",
        &format!("\":{}\"", args.base_port),
    );
    set_config(
        &mut cmd,
        "core.network.protocol.tcp.listen_address",
        &format!("\":{}\"", args.base_port),
    );

    set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.detect_address_changes", "false");
    set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.protocol.ws.listen", "false");

    // Routing table limits — high values for full mesh connectivity in large devnets
    set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.routing_table.limit_over_attached", "200");
    set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.routing_table.limit_fully_attached", "64");
    set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.routing_table.limit_attached_strong", "32");
    set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.routing_table.limit_attached_good", "16");
    set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.routing_table.limit_attached_weak", "2");

    if is_bootstrap {
        set_config(
            &mut cmd,
            "core.capabilities.disable",
            "[\"TUNL\",\"SGNL\",\"RLAY\",\"DIAL\",\"DHTV\",\"DHTW\",\"APPM\"]",
        );
        set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.dht.min_peer_count", "2");
    } else {
        set_config(&mut cmd, "core.capabilities.disable", "[\"APPM\"]");
        set_config(&mut cmd, "core.network.dht.min_peer_count", "10");
    }

    let store_dir = args.data_dir.join("store");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&store_dir).expect("failed to create store dir");
    set_config(
        &mut cmd,
        "core.protected_store.directory",
        &format!("\"{}\"", store_dir.display()),
    );
    set_config(
        &mut cmd,
        "core.table_store.directory",
        &format!("\"{}\"", store_dir.display()),
    );
    set_config(
        &mut cmd,
        "core.block_store.directory",
        &format!("\"{}\"", store_dir.display()),
    );

    let ipc_dir = args.data_dir.join("ipc");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&ipc_dir).expect("failed to create ipc dir");
    set_config(
        &mut cmd,
        "client_api.ipc_directory",
        &format!("\"{}\"", ipc_dir.display()),
    );

    if args.debug {
        cmd.arg("--debug");
    }

    cmd.env("LD_PRELOAD", ipspoof);
    cmd.env("VEILID_BASE_PORT", args.base_port.to_string());

    let log_dir = args.data_dir.join("logs");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&log_dir).expect("failed to create log dir");
    let stdout_file = std::fs::File::create(log_dir.join(format!("node-{index}.stdout")))
        .expect("failed to create stdout log");
    let stderr_file = std::fs::File::create(log_dir.join(format!("node-{index}.stderr")))
        .expect("failed to create stderr log");
    cmd.stdout(stdout_file);
    cmd.stderr(stderr_file);

    let child = cmd.spawn().unwrap_or_else(|e| {
        eprintln!("error: failed to spawn veilid-server for node {index}: {e}");
        std::process::exit(1);
    });

    NodeInfo {
        index,
        port,
        fake_ip,
        child,
    }
}

fn write_state_file(args: &StartArgs, ipspoof: &Path) {
    let ipspoof_abs = std::fs::canonicalize(ipspoof).unwrap_or_else(|_| ipspoof.to_path_buf());
    let env_path = args.data_dir.join("devnet.env");
    let contents = format!(
        "NETWORK_KEY={}\nBASE_PORT={}\nNODE_COUNT={}\nIPSPOOF={}\n",
        args.network_key,
        args.base_port,
        args.nodes,
        ipspoof_abs.display(),
    );
    std::fs::write(&env_path, contents).expect("failed to write devnet.env");

    let slot_path = args.data_dir.join("next_exec_slot");
    std::fs::write(&slot_path, args.nodes.to_string()).expect("failed to write next_exec_slot");
}

fn run_exec(args: &ExecArgs) -> ! {
    use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;

    let env_path = args.data_dir.join("devnet.env");
    let env_contents = std::fs::read_to_string(&env_path).unwrap_or_else(|e| {
        eprintln!(
            "error: cannot read {}: {e}\nIs the playground running?",
            env_path.display()
        );
        std::process::exit(1);
    });

    let mut network_key = String::new();
    let mut base_port: u16 = 0;
    let mut ipspoof = String::new();
    for line in env_contents.lines() {
        if let Some((k, v)) = line.split_once('=') {
            match k {
                "NETWORK_KEY" => network_key = v.to_string(),
                "BASE_PORT" => {
                    base_port = v.parse().unwrap_or_else(|_| {
                        eprintln!("error: invalid BASE_PORT in devnet.env: {v}");
                        std::process::exit(1);
                    });
                }
                "IPSPOOF" => ipspoof = v.to_string(),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }

    if network_key.is_empty() || base_port == 0 || ipspoof.is_empty() {
        eprintln!("error: devnet.env is missing required fields");
        std::process::exit(1);
    }

    let slot_path = args.data_dir.join("next_exec_slot");
    let slot_str = std::fs::read_to_string(&slot_path).unwrap_or_else(|e| {
        eprintln!("error: cannot read {}: {e}", slot_path.display());
        std::process::exit(1);
    });
    let slot: u16 = slot_str.trim().parse().unwrap_or_else(|_| {
        eprintln!(
            "error: invalid slot number in {}: {slot_str}",
            slot_path.display()
        );
        std::process::exit(1);
    });
    std::fs::write(&slot_path, (slot + 1).to_string()).expect("failed to update next_exec_slot");

    let port = base_port + slot;
    let fake_ip = format!("1.2.3.{}", slot + 1);
    let bootstrap = format!("udp://1.2.3.1:{base_port}");

    eprintln!("[playground] exec slot {slot}: {fake_ip}:{port}");

    let err = Command::new(&args.cmd[0])
        .args(&args.cmd[1..])
        .env("LD_PRELOAD", &ipspoof)
        .env("VEILID_BASE_PORT", base_port.to_string())
        .env("VEILID_NETWORK_KEY", &network_key)
        .env("VEILID_BOOTSTRAP", &bootstrap)
        .env("VEILID_PUBLIC_ADDRESS", format!("{fake_ip}:{port}"))
        .env("VEILID_LISTEN_PORT", port.to_string())
        .env("VEILID_NODE_OFFSET", slot.to_string())
        .exec();

    eprintln!("error: exec failed: {err}");
    std::process::exit(1);
}

fn set_config(cmd: &mut Command, key: &str, value: &str) {
    cmd.arg("--set-config").arg(format!("{key}={value}"));
}

fn wait_for_port(port: u16, timeout: Duration, shutdown: &AtomicBool) -> bool {
    let start = Instant::now();
    while start.elapsed() < timeout {
        if shutdown.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            return false;
        }
        if TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).is_ok() {
            return true;
        }
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(250));
    }
    false
}

fn cleanup(nodes: &mut [NodeInfo]) {
    for node in nodes.iter() {
        #[allow(clippy::cast_possible_wrap)]
        let pid = Pid::from_raw(node.child.id() as i32);
        let _ = signal::kill(pid, Signal::SIGTERM);
    }

    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    for node in nodes.iter_mut() {
        let remaining = deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
        if remaining.is_zero() {
            break;
        }
        let poll_start = Instant::now();
        while poll_start.elapsed() < remaining {
            match node.child.try_wait() {
                Ok(Some(_)) => break,
                _ => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)),
            }
        }
    }

    for node in nodes.iter_mut() {
        if !matches!(node.child.try_wait(), Ok(Some(_))) {
            eprintln!(
                "[playground] killing node {} (pid {})",
                node.index,
                node.child.id()
            );
            let _ = node.child.kill();
            let _ = node.child.wait();
        }
    }
}

fn find_in_path(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
    if let Ok(exe) = std::env::current_exe() {
        if let Some(dir) = exe.parent() {
            let candidate = dir.join(name);
            if candidate.exists() {
                return candidate;
            }
        }
    }

    for profile in &["release", "debug"] {
        let candidate = PathBuf::from(format!("target/{profile}/{name}"));
        if candidate.exists() {
            return candidate;
        }
    }

    if let Ok(path) = std::env::var("PATH") {
        for dir in path.split(':') {
            let candidate = PathBuf::from(dir).join(name);
            if candidate.exists() {
                return candidate;
            }
        }
    }
    PathBuf::from(name)
}

fn auto_detect_ipspoof() -> PathBuf {
    if let Ok(exe) = std::env::current_exe() {
        if let Some(dir) = exe.parent() {
            let candidate = dir.join("libveilid_ipspoof.so");
            if candidate.exists() {
                return candidate;
            }
            let candidate = dir
                .parent()
                .map(|p| p.join("release").join("libveilid_ipspoof.so"));
            if let Some(c) = candidate {
                if c.exists() {
                    return c;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    let cwd_candidates = [
        "target/release/libveilid_ipspoof.so",
        "target/debug/libveilid_ipspoof.so",
    ];
    for c in &cwd_candidates {
        let p = PathBuf::from(c);
        if p.exists() {
            return p;
        }
    }

    PathBuf::from("libveilid_ipspoof.so")
}

fn ctrlc_handler(shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>) {
    let _ = ctrlc::set_handler(move || {
        shutdown.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
    });
}

FILE: Repos/veilid/ipspoof/src/lib.rs

//! `LD_PRELOAD` library for Veilid devnet IP translation
//!
//! Enables local multi-node Veilid development by translating between
//! fake "global" IPs (1.2.3.x) and localhost addresses.
//!
//! Each devnet node advertises a fake global IP (1.2.3.1, 1.2.3.2, etc.)
//! but actually runs on localhost with different ports. This library
//! intercepts socket calls to perform the translation transparently.
//!
//! Environment variables:
//! - `VEILID_BASE_PORT`: Base port (default 5150)

use std::sync::OnceLock;

/// `AF_INET` as `sa_family_t`, avoiding a truncation cast on every use.
#[allow(clippy::cast_possible_truncation)] // AF_INET (2) always fits u16
const AF_INET_FAMILY: libc::sa_family_t = libc::AF_INET as libc::sa_family_t;

/// Fake global IP base: 1.2.3.0/24 (host byte order)
const FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE: u32 = 0x0102_0300;
const FAKE_GLOBAL_MASK: u32 = 0xFFFF_FF00;

/// Maximum port offset from base (254 nodes max, limited by 1.2.3.x/24)
const MAX_PORT_OFFSET: u16 = 254;

struct Config {
    base_port: u16,
}

fn config() -> &'static Config {
    static CFG: OnceLock<Config> = OnceLock::new();
    CFG.get_or_init(|| {
        let base_port = std::env::var("VEILID_BASE_PORT")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| s.parse::<u16>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(5150);

        eprintln!("[ipspoof] loaded — translating 1.2.3.x <-> 127.0.0.1 (base port: {base_port})");

        Config { base_port }
    })
}

const fn is_fake_global(addr_host: u32) -> bool {
    (addr_host & FAKE_GLOBAL_MASK) == FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE
}

const fn is_loopback(addr_host: u32) -> bool {
    (addr_host & 0xFF00_0000) == 0x7F00_0000
}

fn is_devnet_port(port: u16) -> bool {
    let bp = config().base_port;
    port >= bp && port < bp + MAX_PORT_OFFSET
}

fn port_to_ip_suffix(port: u16) -> u8 {
    if is_devnet_port(port) {
        u8::try_from(port - config().base_port + 1).unwrap_or(0)
    } else {
        0
    }
}

const fn is_excluded_port(port: u16) -> bool {
    port == 53 || port == 5353 // DNS and mDNS
}

fn socklen_of<T>() -> libc::socklen_t {
    libc::socklen_t::try_from(std::mem::size_of::<T>()).expect("type size fits socklen_t")
}

unsafe fn read_sockaddr_in(addr: *const libc::sockaddr) -> Option<(u32, u16)> {
    if addr.is_null() {
        return None;
    }
    if unsafe { (*addr).sa_family } != AF_INET_FAMILY {
        return None;
    }
    let sin = unsafe { std::ptr::read_unaligned(addr.cast::<libc::sockaddr_in>()) };
    let addr_host = u32::from_be(sin.sin_addr.s_addr);
    let port = u16::from_be(sin.sin_port);
    Some((addr_host, port))
}

unsafe fn write_sockaddr_in_ip(addr: *mut libc::sockaddr, ip_host: u32) {
    let mut sin = unsafe { std::ptr::read_unaligned(addr.cast::<libc::sockaddr_in>()) };
    sin.sin_addr.s_addr = ip_host.to_be();
    unsafe { std::ptr::write_unaligned(addr.cast::<libc::sockaddr_in>(), sin) };
}

/// Rewrite loopback source to fake global: 127.0.0.1:PORT -> 1.2.3.X:PORT
unsafe fn rewrite_recv_addr(addr: *mut libc::sockaddr) {
    let Some((addr_host, port)) = (unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(addr) }) else {
        return;
    };
    if !is_loopback(addr_host) {
        return;
    }
    if is_excluded_port(port) {
        return;
    }
    let suffix = port_to_ip_suffix(port);
    if suffix == 0 {
        return; // ephemeral port, don't translate
    }
    let fake_ip = FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | u32::from(suffix);
    unsafe { write_sockaddr_in_ip(addr, fake_ip) };
}

/// Like `rewrite_recv_addr`, but uses suffix 99 for ephemeral source ports
/// (e.g. BOOT protocol's unbound sockets) so veilid sees a global IP.
unsafe fn rewrite_recv_addr_force(addr: *mut libc::sockaddr) {
    let Some((addr_host, port)) = (unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(addr) }) else {
        return;
    };
    if !is_loopback(addr_host) || is_excluded_port(port) {
        return;
    }
    let suffix = port_to_ip_suffix(port);
    let suffix = if suffix == 0 { 99 } else { suffix };
    let fake_ip = FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | u32::from(suffix);
    unsafe { write_sockaddr_in_ip(addr, fake_ip) };
}

/// Rewrite fake global destination to loopback: 1.2.3.X:PORT -> 127.0.0.1:PORT
unsafe fn rewrite_send_addr(addr: *mut libc::sockaddr) {
    let Some((addr_host, _port)) = (unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(addr) }) else {
        return;
    };
    if !is_fake_global(addr_host) {
        return;
    }
    unsafe { write_sockaddr_in_ip(addr, 0x7F00_0001) };
}

unsafe fn get_local_port(sockfd: libc::c_int) -> u16 {
    let mut local_addr: libc::sockaddr_storage = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
    let mut local_len: libc::socklen_t = socklen_of::<libc::sockaddr_storage>();
    let ret = unsafe {
        redhook::real!(getsockname)(
            sockfd,
            (&raw mut local_addr).cast::<libc::sockaddr>(),
            &raw mut local_len,
        )
    };
    if ret != 0 {
        return 0;
    }
    if local_addr.ss_family == AF_INET_FAMILY {
        let sin = unsafe {
            std::ptr::read_unaligned((&raw const local_addr).cast::<libc::sockaddr_in>())
        };
        u16::from_be(sin.sin_port)
    } else {
        0
    }
}

redhook::hook! {
    unsafe fn recvfrom(
        sockfd: libc::c_int,
        buf: *mut libc::c_void,
        len: libc::size_t,
        flags: libc::c_int,
        src_addr: *mut libc::sockaddr,
        addrlen: *mut libc::socklen_t
    ) -> libc::ssize_t => ipspoof_recvfrom {
        let _ = config();

        let ret = unsafe { redhook::real!(recvfrom)(sockfd, buf, len, flags, src_addr, addrlen) };
        if ret >= 0 && !src_addr.is_null() {
            let local_port = unsafe { get_local_port(sockfd) };
            if is_devnet_port(local_port) {
                unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr_force(src_addr) };
            } else {
                unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(src_addr) };
            }
        }
        ret
    }
}

redhook::hook! {
    unsafe fn sendto(
        sockfd: libc::c_int,
        buf: *const libc::c_void,
        len: libc::size_t,
        flags: libc::c_int,
        dest_addr: *const libc::sockaddr,
        addrlen: libc::socklen_t
    ) -> libc::ssize_t => ipspoof_sendto {
        let _ = config();

        if !dest_addr.is_null() {
            if let Some((addr_host, _port)) = unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(dest_addr) } {
                if is_fake_global(addr_host) {
                    let mut modified = unsafe {
                        std::ptr::read_unaligned(dest_addr.cast::<libc::sockaddr_in>())
                    };
                    unsafe {
                        rewrite_send_addr((&raw mut modified).cast::<libc::sockaddr>());
                        return redhook::real!(sendto)(
                            sockfd,
                            buf,
                            len,
                            flags,
                            (&raw const modified).cast::<libc::sockaddr>(),
                            addrlen,
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        unsafe { redhook::real!(sendto)(sockfd, buf, len, flags, dest_addr, addrlen) }
    }
}

redhook::hook! {
    unsafe fn recvmsg(
        sockfd: libc::c_int,
        msg: *mut libc::msghdr,
        flags: libc::c_int
    ) -> libc::ssize_t => ipspoof_recvmsg {
        let _ = config();

        let ret = unsafe { redhook::real!(recvmsg)(sockfd, msg, flags) };
        if ret >= 0 && !msg.is_null() {
            let msg_ref = unsafe { &*msg };
            if !msg_ref.msg_name.is_null() {
                let local_port = unsafe { get_local_port(sockfd) };
                if is_devnet_port(local_port) {
                    unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr_force(msg_ref.msg_name.cast::<libc::sockaddr>()) };
                } else {
                    unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(msg_ref.msg_name.cast::<libc::sockaddr>()) };
                }
            }
        }
        ret
    }
}

redhook::hook! {
    unsafe fn sendmsg(
        sockfd: libc::c_int,
        msg: *const libc::msghdr,
        flags: libc::c_int
    ) -> libc::ssize_t => ipspoof_sendmsg {
        let _ = config();

        if !msg.is_null() {
            let msg_ref = unsafe { &*msg };
            if !msg_ref.msg_name.is_null() {
                let addr = msg_ref.msg_name as *const libc::sockaddr;
                if let Some((addr_host, _port)) = unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(addr) } {
                    if is_fake_global(addr_host) {
                        let mut modified_msg: libc::msghdr = unsafe { std::ptr::read(msg) };
                        let mut modified_addr = unsafe {
                            std::ptr::read_unaligned(
                                msg_ref.msg_name.cast::<libc::sockaddr_in>(),
                            )
                        };
                        unsafe {
                            rewrite_send_addr(
                                (&raw mut modified_addr).cast::<libc::sockaddr>(),
                            );
                        }
                        modified_msg.msg_name = (&raw mut modified_addr).cast::<libc::c_void>();
                        return unsafe {
                            redhook::real!(sendmsg)(sockfd, &raw const modified_msg, flags)
                        };
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        unsafe { redhook::real!(sendmsg)(sockfd, msg, flags) }
    }
}

redhook::hook! {
    unsafe fn connect(
        sockfd: libc::c_int,
        addr: *const libc::sockaddr,
        addrlen: libc::socklen_t
    ) -> libc::c_int => ipspoof_connect {
        let _ = config();

        if !addr.is_null() && unsafe { (*addr).sa_family } == AF_INET_FAMILY {
            if let Some((addr_host, dest_port)) = unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(addr) } {
                if is_fake_global(addr_host) && is_devnet_port(dest_port) {
                    let mut sock_type: libc::c_int = 0;
                    let mut optlen: libc::socklen_t =
                        socklen_of::<libc::c_int>();
                    if unsafe {
                        libc::getsockopt(
                            sockfd,
                            libc::SOL_SOCKET,
                            libc::SO_TYPE,
                            (&raw mut sock_type).cast::<libc::c_void>(),
                            &raw mut optlen,
                        )
                    } == 0
                        && sock_type == libc::SOCK_STREAM
                    {
                        let bound_port = unsafe { get_local_port(sockfd) };
                        if !is_devnet_port(bound_port) {
                            let reuse: libc::c_int = 1;
                            unsafe {
                                libc::setsockopt(
                                    sockfd,
                                    libc::SOL_SOCKET,
                                    libc::SO_REUSEADDR,
                                    (&raw const reuse).cast::<libc::c_void>(),
                                    socklen_of::<libc::c_int>(),
                                );
                            }
                            let cfg = config();
                            for offset in 0..MAX_PORT_OFFSET {
                                let try_port = cfg.base_port + offset;
                                let mut bind_addr: libc::sockaddr_in =
                                    unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
                                bind_addr.sin_family = AF_INET_FAMILY;
                                bind_addr.sin_addr.s_addr = 0x7F00_0001_u32.to_be();
                                bind_addr.sin_port = try_port.to_be();
                                if unsafe {
                                    libc::bind(
                                        sockfd,
                                        (&raw const bind_addr).cast::<libc::sockaddr>(),
                                        socklen_of::<libc::sockaddr_in>(),
                                    )
                                } == 0
                                {
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    let mut modified = unsafe {
                        std::ptr::read_unaligned(addr.cast::<libc::sockaddr_in>())
                    };
                    modified.sin_addr.s_addr = 0x7F00_0001_u32.to_be();
                    return unsafe {
                        redhook::real!(connect)(
                            sockfd,
                            (&raw const modified).cast::<libc::sockaddr>(),
                            addrlen,
                        )
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        unsafe { redhook::real!(connect)(sockfd, addr, addrlen) }
    }
}

redhook::hook! {
    unsafe fn getpeername(
        sockfd: libc::c_int,
        addr: *mut libc::sockaddr,
        addrlen: *mut libc::socklen_t
    ) -> libc::c_int => ipspoof_getpeername {
        let _ = config();

        let ret = unsafe { redhook::real!(getpeername)(sockfd, addr, addrlen) };
        if ret == 0 && !addr.is_null() {
            let local_port = unsafe { get_local_port(sockfd) };
            if is_devnet_port(local_port) {
                unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr_force(addr) };
            } else {
                unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(addr) };
            }
        }
        ret
    }
}

redhook::hook! {
    unsafe fn getsockname(
        sockfd: libc::c_int,
        addr: *mut libc::sockaddr,
        addrlen: *mut libc::socklen_t
    ) -> libc::c_int => ipspoof_getsockname {
        let _ = config();

        let ret = unsafe { redhook::real!(getsockname)(sockfd, addr, addrlen) };
        if ret == 0 && !addr.is_null() {
            unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(addr) };
        }
        ret
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_is_fake_global() {
        assert!(is_fake_global(0x0102_0301)); // 1.2.3.1
        assert!(is_fake_global(0x0102_0314)); // 1.2.3.20
        assert!(is_fake_global(0x0102_0363)); // 1.2.3.99
        assert!(!is_fake_global(0x7F00_0001)); // 127.0.0.1
        assert!(!is_fake_global(0x0102_0401)); // 1.2.4.1
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_is_loopback() {
        assert!(is_loopback(0x7F00_0001)); // 127.0.0.1
        assert!(is_loopback(0x7F00_0002)); // 127.0.0.2
        assert!(is_loopback(0x7FFF_FFFF)); // 127.255.255.255
        assert!(!is_loopback(0x0A00_0001)); // 10.0.0.1
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_port_to_ip_suffix() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        assert_eq!(port_to_ip_suffix(bp), 1);
        assert_eq!(port_to_ip_suffix(bp + 1), 2);
        assert_eq!(port_to_ip_suffix(bp + 19), 20);
        assert_eq!(port_to_ip_suffix(bp + 39), 40);
        assert_eq!(port_to_ip_suffix(bp + 253), 254); // max
        assert_eq!(port_to_ip_suffix(bp + 254), 0); // out of range
        assert_eq!(port_to_ip_suffix(80), 0); // unrelated port
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_is_devnet_port() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        assert!(is_devnet_port(bp));
        assert!(is_devnet_port(bp + 39));
        assert!(is_devnet_port(bp + 253)); // max
        assert!(!is_devnet_port(bp + 254));
        assert!(!is_devnet_port(80));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_is_excluded_port() {
        assert!(is_excluded_port(53));
        assert!(is_excluded_port(5353));
        assert!(!is_excluded_port(5150));
        assert!(!is_excluded_port(80));
    }

    fn make_sockaddr_in(ip_host: u32, port: u16) -> libc::sockaddr_in {
        let mut sin: libc::sockaddr_in = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
        sin.sin_family = AF_INET_FAMILY;
        sin.sin_addr.s_addr = ip_host.to_be();
        sin.sin_port = port.to_be();
        sin
    }

    fn read_back(sin: &libc::sockaddr_in) -> (u32, u16) {
        (
            u32::from_be(sin.sin_addr.s_addr),
            u16::from_be(sin.sin_port),
        )
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_read_sockaddr_in_valid_ipv4() {
        let sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x0102_0301, 5150);
        let result = unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(&sin as *const _ as *const libc::sockaddr) };
        assert_eq!(result, Some((0x0102_0301, 5150)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_read_sockaddr_in_null() {
        let result = unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(std::ptr::null()) };
        assert_eq!(result, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_read_sockaddr_in_ipv6_rejected() {
        let mut sin6: libc::sockaddr_in6 = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
        sin6.sin6_family = libc::AF_INET6 as libc::sa_family_t;
        let result = unsafe { read_sockaddr_in(&sin6 as *const _ as *const libc::sockaddr) };
        assert_eq!(result, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_rewrite_loopback_to_fake_global() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, bp);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | 1);
        assert_eq!(port, bp);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_rewrite_port_offset_mapping() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, bp + 19);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | 20);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_rewrite_max_offset() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, bp + 39);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | 40);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_noop_on_non_loopback() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x0A00_0001, bp);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x0A00_0001);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_noop_on_non_devnet_port() {
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, 80);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_noop_on_dns_port() {
        let bp = config().base_port;

        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, 53);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);

        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, 5353);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);

        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, bp);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_ne!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_noop_on_port_just_past_range() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, bp + MAX_PORT_OFFSET);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_force_devnet_port() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, bp + 3);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr_force(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | 4);
        assert_eq!(port, bp + 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_force_ephemeral_port_uses_suffix_99() {
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, 45123);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr_force(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | 99);
        assert_eq!(port, 45123);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_force_noop_on_non_loopback() {
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x0A00_0001, 45123);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr_force(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x0A00_0001);
        assert_eq!(port, 45123);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recv_force_noop_on_dns() {
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, 53);
        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr_force(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_send_rewrite_fake_global_to_loopback() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | 1, bp);
        unsafe { rewrite_send_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
        assert_eq!(port, bp);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_send_rewrite_any_suffix() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | 20, bp + 19);
        unsafe { rewrite_send_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
        assert_eq!(port, bp + 19);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_send_noop_on_loopback() {
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, 5150);
        unsafe { rewrite_send_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_send_noop_on_real_public_ip() {
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x0808_0808, 443);
        unsafe { rewrite_send_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x0808_0808);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_roundtrip_recv_then_send() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, bp + 5);

        unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | 6);
        assert_eq!(port, bp + 5);

        unsafe { rewrite_send_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
        assert_eq!(port, bp + 5);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_roundtrip_all_20_devnet_nodes() {
        let bp = config().base_port;
        for i in 0..20u16 {
            let port = bp + i;
            let expected_suffix = (i + 1) as u32;

            let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, port);
            unsafe { rewrite_recv_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
            let (ip, _) = read_back(&sin);
            assert_eq!(ip, FAKE_GLOBAL_BASE | expected_suffix);

            unsafe { rewrite_send_addr(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr) };
            let (ip, p) = read_back(&sin);
            assert_eq!(ip, 0x7F00_0001);
            assert_eq!(p, port);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_write_preserves_port() {
        let mut sin = make_sockaddr_in(0x7F00_0001, 5150);
        unsafe { write_sockaddr_in_ip(&mut sin as *mut _ as *mut libc::sockaddr, 0x0102_0301) };
        let (ip, port) = read_back(&sin);
        assert_eq!(ip, 0x0102_0301);
        assert_eq!(port, 5150);
    }
}
